> **Note:**
> - **ΔRank**: Delta Rank - Average change in ranking position (higher is better)
> - **NRG**: Normalized Ranking Gain (ΔRank / max_citations)
> - **Success@K**: Proportion of samples where boosted items appear in top K%
> - **Promote@K**: Proportion of items promoted from outside top-K to inside top-K
> - **MRR Improvement**: Mean Reciprocal Rank improvement over baseline
> - **KVR**: Keyword Violation Rate (proportion of texts with promotional keywords)
> - **P-value**: Statistical significance (* p<0.05, ** p<0.01, *** p<0.001)
